{"parentUuid":"7a78ed70-364b-41cc-9bae-593d887928b1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_016gXyBp9nCuFS9jYyBcxrFu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FXoKLxT2KMV4aDdm7AFQjD","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\specs\\tier2-feature\\001-validate-investment-thesis.md"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":6796,"cache_creation_input_tokens":41501,"cache_read_input_tokens":35474,"output_tokens":2908,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":41501,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":6796,"output_tokens":2908,"cache_read_input_tokens":35474,"cache_creation_input_tokens":41501,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":41501},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaZqjoUdtn8UqM6oyvfot","type":"assistant","uuid":"c8e227df-a41f-4018-a3a8-74fa5fc2a9e5","timestamp":"2026-04-30T11:01:14.262Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"d97632a7-61c5-43c2-9039-fd6ba70fbfee","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"f3924ec7-f585-4e42-93a8-cf03010b3074","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"0e831e8a-4c8d-4f72-819f-519f0d2f6fbe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01F89TuvNPbyaD8FupZV5V7o","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tspec_id: SPEC-2026-04-23-T1-001\n3\ttier: 1\n4\tstatus: approved\n5\tversion: 1.0\n6\tcreated: 2026-04-23\n7\tlast_reviewed: 2026-04-23\n8\tauthors: [project-owner]\n9\tbounded_contexts: [Market Data, News Stream, Influence Network, Crowd Sentiment, Analysis]\n10\trelated_specs: [SPEC-2026-04-23-001, SPEC-2026-04-23-002, SPEC-2026-04-23-003, SPEC-2026-04-23-004, SPEC-2026-04-23-005]\n11\tubiquitous_language_terms: [Signal Tier, Lead Time, Reliability, Calibration, Hard Data, Official Narrative, Influence Network, Crowd Sentiment, Narrative Phase, Signal Confluence]\n12\t---\n13\t\n14\t# SPEC: Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n15\t\n16\t> **TIER 1 STRATEGIC SPEC** — defines the foundational analytical framework all feature specs build upon.\n17\t\n18\t---\n19\t\n20\t# PART A — STRATEGIC CONTEXT\n21\t\n22\t## A.1 The Insight This Spec Encodes\n23\t\n24\tQuant funds in efficient markets win on three things: clean data, execution speed, and diverse alpha factors. They explicitly avoid sentiment from social media because it's noise at their scale and gets arbitraged away in efficient markets.\n25\t\n26\tVietnam stock market inverts every assumption:\n27\t- **Inefficient by structure**: 85-90% retail investor share (US: 20-25%); information asymmetry is structural, not transient\n28\t- **Sentiment is signal, not noise**: KOL recommendations measurably move prices; pump operators visible\n29\t- **Narrative dominates fundamentals**: stocks can deviate from fair value for 6-24 months based on which narrative captures attention\n30\t- **Foreign flow distorts**: $1B can move VN-Index 5-7%; same $1B moves S&P 500 by 0.0001%\n31\t\n32\tThe arbitrage opportunity: **apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN**.\n33\t\n34\tThis spec defines HOW we encode that opportunity into a four-tier signal architecture that every other feature builds on.\n35\t\n36\t## A.2 The Four Tiers\n37\t\n38\tEach tier has fundamentally different properties:\n39\t\n40\t| Tier | Type | Source | Lead Time | Reliability | LLM Role |\n41\t|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n42\t| **1** Hard Data | Deterministic | Exchange feeds, financial reports | Hours-Days | Very High | None |\n43\t| **2** Official Narrative | Semi-structured | Mainstream news, broker reports | Real-time | High | Extract claims |\n44\t| **3** Influence Network | Semi-structured | YouTube, KOL pages, podcasts | Can lead by days | Medium (per-KOL calibrated) | Transcribe + extract |\n45\t| **4** Crowd Sentiment | Unstructured | Forums, public chats, comments | Lags price | Low (mass behavior) | Classify + detect coordination |\n46\t\n47\tCritical: tiers are not \"more data is better\". They are **different kinds of information** with different decision implications.\n48\t\n49\t## A.3 The Combinatorial Edge\n50\t\n51\tThe edge is not in any single tier. It's in **how tiers combine**.\n52\t\n53\t**Pattern 1: Early Opportunity (T1+T2+T3 confluence before T4)**\n54\t- Hard data shows fundamentals improving (Q-on-Q margin expansion, inventory normalizing)\n55\t- Official news quietly reports positive developments (broker quietly upgrades, no fanfare)\n56\t- 2-3 calibrated KOLs start mentioning the stock with conviction\n57\t- BUT crowd sentiment hasn't woken up yet (low mention frequency, no FOMO posts)\n58\t- → **High-confidence early entry signal**\n59\t\n60\t**Pattern 2: Likely Top (T4 hot but T1+T2 weak)**\n61\t- Crowd sentiment frenzied (mention frequency at 90th percentile, FOMO posts dominant)\n62\t- Multiple KOLs pushing the stock (T3 hot)\n63\t- BUT hard data shows decelerating fundamentals (T1 weak)\n64\t- AND no major positive official news (T2 silent)\n65\t- → **Likely distribution phase, avoid or exit**\n66\t\n67\t**Pattern 3: Manufactured Pump (T3 hot, T4 building, T1+T2 absent)**\n68\t- Few specific KOLs (often same ones repeatedly) push stock\n69\t- Crowd starts repeating same talking points (coordination signature)\n70\t- No fundamental basis (T1)\n71\t- No real news (T2)\n72\t- → **Pump warning, avoid entirely**\n73\t\n74\t**Pattern 4: Forgotten Value (T1 strong, T2-T4 silent)**\n75\t- Excellent fundamentals quietly grinding\n76\t- No news catalysts\n77\t- No KOL attention\n78\t- No crowd buzz\n79\t- → **Value trap candidate OR patient accumulation opportunity (need catalyst hypothesis)**\n80\t\n81\tThese patterns are detectable only when you track all four tiers with calibrated reliability scores.\n82\t\n83\t## A.4 Why This Beats Single-Tier Approaches\n84\t\n85\t**Approach: Pure Tier 1 (traditional quant)**\n86\t- Misses narrative-driven mispricing that lasts months in VN\n87\t- Gets value-trapped in cheap stocks no one cares about\n88\t- Cannot detect or avoid pumps\n89\t\n90\t**Approach: Pure Tier 2 (news-based)**\n91\t- News is reactive, often coincident or lagging\n92\t- Misses pre-news positioning\n93\t- Susceptible to \"official narrative\" being co-opted\n94\t\n95\t**Approach: Pure Tier 3 (follow KOLs)**\n96\t- KOL accuracy varies wildly (5%-70% hit rate observed)\n97\t- KOL recommendations susceptible to incentive bias (paid promotions)\n98\t- Reactive — by time KOL public, edge often gone\n99\t\n100\t**Approach: Pure Tier 4 (sentiment trading)**\n101\t- Crowd sentiment lags; trading on it = late\n102\t- High signal-to-noise ratio without filtering\n103\t- Pumpers manipulate sentiment deliberately\n104\t\n105\t**Combined four-tier approach:**\n106\t- Each tier validates or contradicts the others\n107\t- Confluence = high confidence; disagreement = caution flag\n108\t- Pump operators can't manipulate all four tiers simultaneously (T1 hardest to fake)\n109\t\n110\t## A.5 The Compounding Moat\n111\t\n112\tThis architecture creates compounding moats over time:\n113\t\n114\t**Per-tier calibration**:\n115\t- Tier 1 doesn't need calibration (deterministic)\n116\t- Tier 2 needs source calibration (which broker reports prove right? which news outlets best for which sectors?)\n117\t- Tier 3 needs per-KOL calibration (each KOL gets a credibility score that updates monthly)\n118\t- Tier 4 needs pattern calibration (which crowd patterns precede tops/bottoms?)\n119\t\n120\tAfter 12 months, calibration data becomes proprietary. After 24 months, it's irreplicable without starting over.\n121\t\n122\t**Cross-tier pattern library**:\n123\t- \"Pattern X happened 7 times since 2022. 5 were profitable. 2 false signals had common feature Y.\"\n124\t- Each new occurrence updates the pattern's reliability score.\n125\t- Library starts empty; grows with every dogfood session.\n126\t\n127\t---\n128\t\n129\t# PART B — TECHNICAL ARCHITECTURE\n130\t\n131\t## B.1 Tier 1: Hard Data\n132\t\n133\t### Sources\n134\t\n135\t**Required (Phase 1)**:\n136\t- vnstock — historical EOD prices, OHLCV, basic fundamentals\n137\t- Vietstock public — financial reports (PDF + structured)\n138\t- TCBS API — semi-public quotes, foreign flow\n139\t- VnDirect / SSI public APIs — backup sources\n140\t\n141\t**Optional (Phase 2-3 if budget)**:\n142\t- FiinPro — comprehensive normalized fundamentals\n143\t- Bloomberg / Refinitiv — overkill for personal use\n144\t\n145\t### Data Categories\n146\t\n147\t```python\n148\t# Stored in BC-1 Market Data\n149\tclass Quote:        # Time-series, TimescaleDB hypertable\n150\t    ticker: Ticker\n151\t    timestamp: datetime\n152\t    open: Money\n153\t    high: Money\n154\t    low: Money\n155\t    close: Money\n156\t    volume: int\n157\t    foreign_buy: int\n158\t    foreign_sell: int\n159\t    adjustment_type: AdjustmentType\n160\t    source_provider: SourceProvider\n161\t\n162\t# Stored in BC-2 Fundamental\n163\tclass FinancialStatement:\n164\t    ticker: Ticker\n165\t    statement_type: StatementType  # IS | BS | CF\n166\t    period_end: date\n167\t    filing_date: date\n168\t    line_items: dict[str, Money]\n169\t    source_provider: SourceProvider\n170\t\n171\t# Stored in BC-4 Macro\n172\tclass MacroIndicator:\n173\t    indicator: str  # \"VN_GDP_GROWTH\", \"VN_CPI\", \"FED_FUNDS_RATE\"\n174\t    timestamp: datetime\n175\t    value: Decimal\n176\t    unit: str\n177\t```\n178\t\n179\t### LLM Role: NONE\n180\t\n181\tTier 1 is pure code. LLM never touches it. Calculations like P/E, ROE, DCF use deterministic formulas in `packages/domain/fundamental/services/`.\n182\t\n183\t### Lead Time\n184\t\n185\t- Intraday: real-time (if subscribed) or 15-min delay\n186\t- EOD: end of trading day\n187\t- Fundamentals: filing date + ingestion lag (typically T+1 day)\n188\t\n189\t### Reliability\n190\t\n191\tVery high, BUT:\n192\t- Source disagreements happen (Rule 4 in financial-data-protocol.md)\n193\t- Restated financials happen (track via `restated_at` field)\n194\t- Adjustment type confusion is biggest risk\n195\t\n196\t## B.2 Tier 2: Official Narrative\n197\t\n198\t### Sources\n199\t\n200\t**Required**:\n201\t- CafeF (cafef.vn) — most popular VN financial news\n202\t- Vietstock News (vietstock.vn) — second most popular\n203\t- NDH (nhipcaudautu.vn) — quality analysis\n204\t- VietnamBiz (vietnambiz.vn) — business focus\n205\t- Đầu Tư Chứng Khoán (tinnhanhchungkhoan.vn)\n206\t\n207\t**Source-specific (companies/sectors)**:\n208\t- Company official announcements via HOSE/HNX disclosure portals\n209\t- Broker reports (SSI, HSC, VCSC, VND, FPTS) — public summaries\n210\t\n211\t**Government/Regulatory**:\n212\t- SBV (State Bank of Vietnam) announcements\n213\t- MoF (Ministry of Finance) policy documents\n214\t- Quốc hội / Chính phủ document portals\n215\t\n216\t### Data Categories\n217\t\n218\t```python\n219\t# Stored in BC-5 News Stream\n220\tclass NewsArticle:\n221\t    article_id: str\n222\t    source: str               # \"cafef\", \"vietstock\", etc\n223\t    url: str\n224\t    title: str\n225\t    body_excerpt: str         # full body in R2\n226\t    published_at: datetime\n227\t    ingested_at: datetime\n228\t    \n229\tclass ExtractedClaim:\n230\t    claim_id: str\n231\t    article_id: str\n232\t    claim_text: str           # extracted by LLM\n233\t    mentioned_tickers: list[Ticker]\n234\t    mentioned_sectors: list[Sector]\n235\t    sentiment: Sentiment      # categorical, not numeric (Rule 7)\n236\t    extractor_model: str\n237\t    extractor_version: str\n238\t    extracted_at: datetime\n239\t    source_text_excerpt: str\n240\t    confidence_extracted: float\n241\t```\n242\t\n243\t### LLM Role\n244\t\n245\t- **Extract structured claims** from unstructured news\n246\t- **Classify sentiment** categorically (STRONGLY_BULLISH | BULLISH | NEUTRAL | BEARISH | STRONGLY_BEARISH)\n247\t- **Identify mentioned entities** (tickers, sectors, people)\n248\t- **Detect contradictions** between sources reporting same event\n249\t- **Cluster related stories** (same event covered by multiple sources)\n250\t\n251\tLLM does NOT:\n252\t- Generate sentiment scores numerically\n253\t- Predict price impact (that's BC-8 Analysis with deterministic models)\n254\t- Produce summaries that displace original (copyright + accuracy)\n255\t\n256\t### Lead Time\n257\t\n258\tReal-time. Pipeline polls every 5-15 min for new articles.\n259\t\n260\t### Reliability\n261\t\n262\tHigh but variable:\n263\t- Major outlets (CafeF, Vietstock): high reliability for facts, watch for \"PR disguised as news\"\n264\t- Smaller outlets: variable; calibrate per source over time\n265\t- Broker reports: have inherent bias (broker has positions); track which broker is right for which sector\n266\t- Government docs: high reliability for facts, low for interpretation\n267\t\n268\t## B.3 Tier 3: Influence Network ⭐\n269\t\n270\t### Sources\n271\t\n272\t**YouTube channels** (selected, not exhaustive — examples):\n273\t- Mr. X30, Học Viện Đầu Tư, Take Profit, Đầu Tư Từ Đầu, Giao Dịch Cùng Tôi, etc.\n274\t- Each channel: subscribe with yt-dlp, monitor for new uploads daily\n275\t\n276\t**Facebook fanpages** (public, ToS-respecting):\n277\t- Fanpages of well-known finance KOLs\n278\t- Read-only access, respect rate limits\n279\t\n280\t**Telegram public channels**:\n281\t- Public broadcast channels (not private groups)\n282\t- Use Telegram API with read-only bot\n283\t\n284\t**Podcasts**:\n285\t- BizPodcast, Money Talk, others\n286\t- RSS feeds + Whisper transcription\n287\t\n288\t**Personal blogs / Substack**:\n289\t- Vietnamese investing bloggers with consistent output\n290\t\n291\t### Data Categories\n292\t\n293\t```python\n294\t# Stored in BC-6 Influence Network\n295\tclass Kol:\n296\t    kol_id: KolId\n297\t    name: str\n298\t    primary_channel: ChannelId\n299\t    secondary_channels: list[ChannelId]\n300\t    profile_summary: str       # human-curated\n301\t    sectors_covered: list[Sector]\n302\t    style: KolStyle           # FUNDAMENTAL | TECHNICAL | NARRATIVE | PUMPER | MIXED\n303\t    credibility_score: CredibilityScore  # see calibration spec\n304\t    \n305\tclass Channel:\n306\t    channel_id: ChannelId\n307\t    platform: Platform        # YOUTUBE | FACEBOOK | TELEGRAM | PODCAST | BLOG\n308\t    url: str\n309\t    kol_id: KolId\n310\t    subscriber_count: int\n311\t    last_scraped_at: datetime\n312\t    \n313\tclass Recommendation:\n314\t    recommendation_id: str\n315\t    kol_id: KolId\n316\t    channel_id: ChannelId\n317\t    source_url: str\n318\t    timestamp_in_source: str   # for video: \"12:34-13:02\"\n319\t    transcript_excerpt: str\n320\t    ticker: Ticker\n321\t    extracted_intent: Intent   # STRONG_BUY | BUY | WATCH | NEUTRAL | AVOID | STRONG_AVOID\n322\t    extracted_conditions: list[str]\n323\t    extracted_timeframe: Timeframe\n324\t    extraction_confidence: float\n325\t    published_at: datetime\n326\t    extracted_at: datetime\n327\t    \n328\tclass CredibilityScore:\n329\t    kol_id: KolId\n330\t    overall_hit_rate: float\n331\t    n_recommendations_evaluated: int\n332\t    sector_specific_scores: dict[Sector, float]\n333\t    timeframe_specific_scores: dict[Timeframe, float]\n334\t    last_updated_at: datetime\n335\t    bayesian_confidence_interval: tuple[float, float]\n336\t```\n337\t\n338\t### LLM Role\n339\t\n340\t- **Transcribe** audio/video (Whisper, not LLM but adjacent)\n341\t- **Extract recommendations** with timestamp, intent, conditions, timeframe\n342\t- **Detect coordinated messaging** (same KOL mentioning many stocks in pattern)\n343\t- **Classify KOL style** (fundamental? technical? narrative-driven? potential pumper?)\n344\t- **Identify when KOL changes view** (was bullish, now bearish — track flip-flop frequency)\n345\t\n346\t### Lead Time\n347\t\n348\tVariable per KOL:\n349\t- Some KOLs lead price by 1-3 days (publish recommendation, audience acts)\n350\t- Some are coincident (publish during the move)\n351\t- Some are contrarian indicators (pumpers; their recommendation = sell signal)\n352\t\n353\tThis is exactly what calibration database tracks.\n354\t\n355\t### Reliability\n356\t\n357\tVery wide range. Each KOL gets calibrated:\n358\t- Some KOLs have 60-70% hit rate → high signal\n359\t- Some have 30-40% → noise\n360\t- Some have <30% → contra-indicator (consistent enough to bet against)\n361\t\n362\tAfter 6 months tracking, you have calibrated weights per KOL. After 12 months, statistically meaningful.\n363\t\n364\tSee `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` for full implementation.\n365\t\n366\t## B.4 Tier 4: Crowd Sentiment & Pump Detection ⭐\n367\t\n368\t### Sources\n369\t\n370\t**Forums (public)**:\n371\t- F319 (forum cổ phiếu)\n372\t- VFPress\n373\t- Comments on CafeF/Vietstock articles\n374\t\n375\t**Facebook public groups** (public, ToS-respecting):\n376\t- \"Đầu tư chứng khoán Việt Nam\" type groups (large public ones)\n377\t- Read-only, respect rate limits\n378\t- NEVER private groups\n379\t\n380\t**Telegram public broadcast channels**:\n381\t- Stock-specific public channels\n382\t- Public chat rooms only\n383\t\n384\t**Twitter/X**:\n385\t- VN finance Twitter (smaller but growing)\n386\t\n387\t### Data Categories\n388\t\n389\t```python\n390\t# Stored in BC-7 Crowd Sentiment\n391\tclass SentimentSnapshot:\n392\t    snapshot_id: str\n393\t    ticker: Ticker\n394\t    captured_at: datetime\n395\t    window: Window               # 1H | 4H | 1D | 1W\n396\t    mention_count: int\n397\t    sentiment_distribution: SentimentDistribution  # categorical counts\n398\t    posting_velocity: float       # posts/hour\n399\t    unique_posters: int\n400\t    coordinated_posting_score: float  # 0-1, see pump detection\n401\t    sources: dict[str, int]       # source -> mention count\n402\t\n403\tclass Narrative:\n404\t    narrative_id: NarrativeId\n405\t    description: str               # \"đầu tư công 2026\"\n406\t    affected_tickers: list[Ticker]\n407\t    affected_sectors: list[Sector]\n408\t    first_detected_at: datetime\n409\t    current_phase: NarrativePhase  # INCUBATION | EMERGING | MAINSTREAM | SATURATION | EXHAUSTION | REVERSAL\n410\t    velocity_trend: str            # ACCELERATING | STEADY | DECELERATING\n411\t    counter_narratives: list[NarrativeId]\n412\t    \n413\tclass PumpDetection:\n414\t    detection_id: str\n415\t    ticker: Ticker\n416\t    detected_at: datetime\n417\t    phase: PumpPhase  # PRE_PUMP | PUMP | DISTRIBUTION | DUMP | UNCERTAIN\n418\t    phase_confidence: float\n419\t    contributing_signals: list[SignalSnapshot]  # what triggered detection\n420\t    historical_similar_cases: list[PumpDetectionId]  # past matches\n421\t```\n422\t\n423\t### LLM Role\n424\t\n425\t- **Classify Vietnamese sentiment** (categorical, not numeric)\n426\t- **Detect coordinated posting** (template detection, timing clustering)\n427\t- **Identify narrative themes** in posts\n428\t- **Extract pump signatures** (specific language patterns)\n429\t- **Compare to historical labeled pump cases**\n430\t\n431\t### Lead Time\n432\t\n433\tTypically LAGS price. Crowd sentiment is mostly exhaustion signal:\n434\t- High crowd activity often = late stage\n435\t- Sentiment euphoria often = top forming\n436\t- Sentiment despair often = bottom forming\n437\t\n438\tUseful primarily as **timing signal** (when narrative-driven move is exhausting), not as **direction signal**.\n439\t\n440\t### Reliability\n441\t\n442\tMass behavior is noisy individually but structured statistically. Patterns reliable, individual posts not.\n443\t\n444\tSee `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` for full implementation.\n445\t\n446\t## B.5 Cross-Tier Synthesis\n447\t\n448\t### The Confluence Engine\n449\t\n450\tCombines tier signals into actionable assessments. Lives in BC-8 Analysis.\n451\t\n452\t```python\n453\tclass ConfluenceAssessment:\n454\t    ticker: Ticker\n455\t    assessed_at: datetime\n456\t    tier_signals: dict[SignalTier, TierSignal]\n457\t    confluence_pattern: ConfluencePattern  # see patterns A.3\n458\t    confluence_strength: float  # how many tiers agree\n459\t    historical_pattern_matches: list[PatternMatch]\n460\t    historical_hit_rate: float | None  # if pattern has enough samples\n461\t    recommended_action: RecommendedAction  # WATCH | INVESTIGATE | THESIS_CANDIDATE | PUMP_AVOID\n462\t    reasoning: str  # LLM-generated, grounded in tier signals\n463\t```\n464\t\n465\t### Decision Rules\n466\t\n467\tPhase 1: Simple rules\n468\t- All 4 tiers agree positive → WATCH (potential thesis)\n469\t- T1+T2+T3 agree, T4 silent → INVESTIGATE (early opportunity)\n470\t- T4 hot, T1 weak → AVOID (potential top)\n471\t- T3 hot from low-credibility KOLs only, T1 weak → PUMP_AVOID\n472\t\n473\tPhase 4: Karpathy-optimized weights\n474\t- Outer loop tunes signal weights based on historical hit rate\n475\t- Same patterns evaluated, but weights evolve\n476\t\n477\t## B.6 Storage Strategy\n478\t\n479\t### Tier 1 (high volume, time-series)\n480\t- TimescaleDB hypertables (price/volume)\n481\t- Postgres regular tables (fundamentals)\n482\t- Compression: TimescaleDB native after 7 days\n483\t- Retention: full history for prices, 10 years for fundamentals\n484\t\n485\t### Tier 2 (medium volume, append-mostly)\n486\t- Postgres for metadata + extracted claims\n487\t- R2 for full article text (cheap storage)\n488\t- pgvector for semantic search across articles\n489\t- Retention: full history (cheap)\n490\t\n491\t### Tier 3 (medium volume, mixed)\n492\t- Postgres for KOL profiles + recommendations + calibration\n493\t- R2 for video/podcast transcripts\n494\t- Retention: full history of recommendations + calibrated outcomes\n495\t\n496\t### Tier 4 (high volume, low individual value)\n497\t- TimescaleDB for sentiment snapshots\n498\t- Postgres for narratives + pump detections\n499\t- R2 for raw posts (sample only, not all)\n500\t- Retention: aggregated retained forever, individual posts 90 days unless flagged\n501\t\n502\t## B.7 Update Cadences\n503\t\n504\t```yaml\n505\t# Defined in apps/data-pipeline/schedules.py\n506\t\n507\ttier_1_hard_data:\n508\t  intraday: every 15 min during market hours (9:00-15:00 VN time)\n509\t  eod: 16:00 daily\n510\t  fundamentals: nightly poll for new filings\n511\t  macro: daily at 8:00\n512\t\n513\ttier_2_official_narrative:\n514\t  news_polling: every 10 min\n515\t  broker_reports: daily at 18:00\n516\t  regulatory: every 4 hours\n517\t\n518\ttier_3_influence_network:\n519\t  youtube_channels: daily at 20:00 (after most channels publish)\n520\t  facebook_fanpages: every 4 hours during active hours\n521\t  podcasts: hourly RSS check\n522\t  blogs: every 2 hours\n523\t\n524\ttier_4_crowd_sentiment:\n525\t  forums: every 30 min\n526\t  facebook_groups: every 2 hours\n527\t  telegram: every 1 hour\n528\t  comments: with parent article fetch\n529\t\n530\tcross_tier:\n531\t  confluence_engine: every 1 hour during market days\n532\t  pattern_matching: nightly at 22:00\n533\t  narrative_lifecycle_update: every 4 hours\n534\t```\n535\t\n536\t## B.8 Cost Profile\n537\t\n538\tEstimated costs for Phase 1 (Tier 1+2 only) for personal use:\n539\t\n540\t```\n541\tData sources: 0-2M VND/month (mostly free + light scraping)\n542\tLLM (Tier 2 extraction): ~5-10M VND/month\n543\tCompute (single VPS): 1-2M VND/month\n544\tStorage (R2): <0.5M VND/month\n545\tTotal: ~10-15M VND/month\n546\t```\n547\t\n548\tPhase 2 adds Tier 3+4:\n549\t```\n550\t+Whisper transcription: 3-5M VND/month\n551\t+LLM (Tier 3+4 extraction): 10-15M VND/month\n552\t+Storage growth: 1-2M VND/month\n553\tTotal: ~25-35M VND/month\n554\t```\n555\t\n556\t## B.9 Privacy & Legal\n557\t\n558\t- All scraping respects robots.txt and reasonable rate limits\n559\t- User agent identifies as research tool with contact email\n560\t- Public sources only — never private groups, never paid leaks\n561\t- Personal data not retained beyond what's needed for analysis\n562\t- GDPR-style: if KOL requests their data removed, comply\n563\t- Output explicitly framed as personal research, never licensed advice\n564\t\n565\t---\n566\t\n567\t# PART C — PROVENANCE & REVIEW\n568\t\n569\t## C.1 Why This Architecture (Not Alternative)\n570\t\n571\t**Considered alternative**: Single-pipeline LLM approach (feed everything to LLM, get verdict)\n572\t- **Rejected because**: black box, no calibration possible, no compounding moat, hallucination risk extreme\n573\t\n574\t**Considered alternative**: Pure quant (no LLM, no sentiment)\n575\t- **Rejected because**: misses VN-specific edge, gets value-trapped, can't detect pumps\n576\t\n577\t**Considered alternative**: Three-tier (combine T3+T4)\n578\t- **Rejected because**: Influence Network and Crowd Sentiment have very different ingestion patterns, calibration logic, and meaning. Combining creates god-package. Separation enables independent evolution.\n579\t\n580\t**Considered alternative**: Five-tier (split T1 into market data + fundamentals)\n581\t- **Rejected because**: They're both deterministic with same LLM role (none); the natural split is BC-1 vs BC-2 in architecture, not two separate signal tiers.\n582\t\n583\t## C.2 Open Questions for Future Revision\n584\t\n585\t- Should there be a Tier 5 for \"insider\" signals (block trades, unusual options activity)? Currently in BC-1 but possibly distinct.\n586\t- Should foreign flow get its own tier? Currently in BC-1 Market Data.\n587\t- How do we handle paid data sources cleanly when they enter (FiinPro)?\n588\t- When does Karpathy outer loop become reliable enough to trust weight updates?\n589\t\n590\t## C.3 Reviews\n591\t\n592\t| Date | Reviewer | Status | Notes |\n593\t|---|---|---|---|\n594\t| 2026-04-23 | project-owner | Approved | Initial spec |\n595\t\n596\t---\n597\t\n598\t## Related Specs\n599\t\n600\t- **Implementation specs (Tier 2)**:\n601\t  - `specs/tier2-feature/001-validate-investment-thesis.md` — uses confluence engine\n602\t  - `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` — implements Tier 3\n603\t  - `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` — implements Tier 4\n604\t  - `specs/tier2-feature/004-multi-perspective-adversarial-agents.md` — synthesizes tiers\n605\t  - `specs/tier2-feature/005-karpathy-outer-loop.md` — optimizes tier weights\n606\t\n607\t- **Constitution**:\n608\t  - `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` — 9 BC structure\n609\t  - `agent-workspace/constitution/financial-data-protocol.md` — data integrity rules\n610\t  - `agent-workspace/constitution/invariants.md` — I-S* stock-specific invariants\n611\t"}]},"uuid":"d4e195d1-a8bc-49d3-b9cc-3aad981a9cf8","timestamp":"2026-04-30T11:01:14.420Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\specs\\tier1-strategic\\001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md","content":"---\nspec_id: SPEC-2026-04-23-T1-001\ntier: 1\nstatus: approved\nversion: 1.0\ncreated: 2026-04-23\nlast_reviewed: 2026-04-23\nauthors: [project-owner]\nbounded_contexts: [Market Data, News Stream, Influence Network, Crowd Sentiment, Analysis]\nrelated_specs: [SPEC-2026-04-23-001, SPEC-2026-04-23-002, SPEC-2026-04-23-003, SPEC-2026-04-23-004, SPEC-2026-04-23-005]\nubiquitous_language_terms: [Signal Tier, Lead Time, Reliability, Calibration, Hard Data, Official Narrative, Influence Network, Crowd Sentiment, Narrative Phase, Signal Confluence]\n---\n\n# SPEC: Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n\n> **TIER 1 STRATEGIC SPEC** — defines the foundational analytical framework all feature specs build upon.\n\n---\n\n# PART A — STRATEGIC CONTEXT\n\n## A.1 The Insight This Spec Encodes\n\nQuant funds in efficient markets win on three things: clean data, execution speed, and diverse alpha factors. They explicitly avoid sentiment from social media because it's noise at their scale and gets arbitraged away in efficient markets.\n\nVietnam stock market inverts every assumption:\n- **Inefficient by structure**: 85-90% retail investor share (US: 20-25%); information asymmetry is structural, not transient\n- **Sentiment is signal, not noise**: KOL recommendations measurably move prices; pump operators visible\n- **Narrative dominates fundamentals**: stocks can deviate from fair value for 6-24 months based on which narrative captures attention\n- **Foreign flow distorts**: $1B can move VN-Index 5-7%; same $1B moves S&P 500 by 0.0001%\n\nThe arbitrage opportunity: **apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN**.\n\nThis spec defines HOW we encode that opportunity into a four-tier signal architecture that every other feature builds on.\n\n## A.2 The Four Tiers\n\nEach tier has fundamentally different properties:\n\n| Tier | Type | Source | Lead Time | Reliability | LLM Role |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **1** Hard Data | Deterministic | Exchange feeds, financial reports | Hours-Days | Very High | None |\n| **2** Official Narrative | Semi-structured | Mainstream news, broker reports | Real-time | High | Extract claims |\n| **3** Influence Network | Semi-structured | YouTube, KOL pages, podcasts | Can lead by days | Medium (per-KOL calibrated) | Transcribe + extract |\n| **4** Crowd Sentiment | Unstructured | Forums, public chats, comments | Lags price | Low (mass behavior) | Classify + detect coordination |\n\nCritical: tiers are not \"more data is better\". They are **different kinds of information** with different decision implications.\n\n## A.3 The Combinatorial Edge\n\nThe edge is not in any single tier. It's in **how tiers combine**.\n\n**Pattern 1: Early Opportunity (T1+T2+T3 confluence before T4)**\n- Hard data shows fundamentals improving (Q-on-Q margin expansion, inventory normalizing)\n- Official news quietly reports positive developments (broker quietly upgrades, no fanfare)\n- 2-3 calibrated KOLs start mentioning the stock with conviction\n- BUT crowd sentiment hasn't woken up yet (low mention frequency, no FOMO posts)\n- → **High-confidence early entry signal**\n\n**Pattern 2: Likely Top (T4 hot but T1+T2 weak)**\n- Crowd sentiment frenzied (mention frequency at 90th percentile, FOMO posts dominant)\n- Multiple KOLs pushing the stock (T3 hot)\n- BUT hard data shows decelerating fundamentals (T1 weak)\n- AND no major positive official news (T2 silent)\n- → **Likely distribution phase, avoid or exit**\n\n**Pattern 3: Manufactured Pump (T3 hot, T4 building, T1+T2 absent)**\n- Few specific KOLs (often same ones repeatedly) push stock\n- Crowd starts repeating same talking points (coordination signature)\n- No fundamental basis (T1)\n- No real news (T2)\n- → **Pump warning, avoid entirely**\n\n**Pattern 4: Forgotten Value (T1 strong, T2-T4 silent)**\n- Excellent fundamentals quietly grinding\n- No news catalysts\n- No KOL attention\n- No crowd buzz\n- → **Value trap candidate OR patient accumulation opportunity (need catalyst hypothesis)**\n\nThese patterns are detectable only when you track all four tiers with calibrated reliability scores.\n\n## A.4 Why This Beats Single-Tier Approaches\n\n**Approach: Pure Tier 1 (traditional quant)**\n- Misses narrative-driven mispricing that lasts months in VN\n- Gets value-trapped in cheap stocks no one cares about\n- Cannot detect or avoid pumps\n\n**Approach: Pure Tier 2 (news-based)**\n- News is reactive, often coincident or lagging\n- Misses pre-news positioning\n- Susceptible to \"official narrative\" being co-opted\n\n**Approach: Pure Tier 3 (follow KOLs)**\n- KOL accuracy varies wildly (5%-70% hit rate observed)\n- KOL recommendations susceptible to incentive bias (paid promotions)\n- Reactive — by time KOL public, edge often gone\n\n**Approach: Pure Tier 4 (sentiment trading)**\n- Crowd sentiment lags; trading on it = late\n- High signal-to-noise ratio without filtering\n- Pumpers manipulate sentiment deliberately\n\n**Combined four-tier approach:**\n- Each tier validates or contradicts the others\n- Confluence = high confidence; disagreement = caution flag\n- Pump operators can't manipulate all four tiers simultaneously (T1 hardest to fake)\n\n## A.5 The Compounding Moat\n\nThis architecture creates compounding moats over time:\n\n**Per-tier calibration**:\n- Tier 1 doesn't need calibration (deterministic)\n- Tier 2 needs source calibration (which broker reports prove right? which news outlets best for which sectors?)\n- Tier 3 needs per-KOL calibration (each KOL gets a credibility score that updates monthly)\n- Tier 4 needs pattern calibration (which crowd patterns precede tops/bottoms?)\n\nAfter 12 months, calibration data becomes proprietary. After 24 months, it's irreplicable without starting over.\n\n**Cross-tier pattern library**:\n- \"Pattern X happened 7 times since 2022. 5 were profitable. 2 false signals had common feature Y.\"\n- Each new occurrence updates the pattern's reliability score.\n- Library starts empty; grows with every dogfood session.\n\n---\n\n# PART B — TECHNICAL ARCHITECTURE\n\n## B.1 Tier 1: Hard Data\n\n### Sources\n\n**Required (Phase 1)**:\n- vnstock — historical EOD prices, OHLCV, basic fundamentals\n- Vietstock public — financial reports (PDF + structured)\n- TCBS API — semi-public quotes, foreign flow\n- VnDirect / SSI public APIs — backup sources\n\n**Optional (Phase 2-3 if budget)**:\n- FiinPro — comprehensive normalized fundamentals\n- Bloomberg / Refinitiv — overkill for personal use\n\n### Data Categories\n\n```python\n# Stored in BC-1 Market Data\nclass Quote:        # Time-series, TimescaleDB hypertable\n    ticker: Ticker\n    timestamp: datetime\n    open: Money\n    high: Money\n    low: Money\n    close: Money\n    volume: int\n    foreign_buy: int\n    foreign_sell: int\n    adjustment_type: AdjustmentType\n    source_provider: SourceProvider\n\n# Stored in BC-2 Fundamental\nclass FinancialStatement:\n    ticker: Ticker\n    statement_type: StatementType  # IS | BS | CF\n    period_end: date\n    filing_date: date\n    line_items: dict[str, Money]\n    source_provider: SourceProvider\n\n# Stored in BC-4 Macro\nclass MacroIndicator:\n    indicator: str  # \"VN_GDP_GROWTH\", \"VN_CPI\", \"FED_FUNDS_RATE\"\n    timestamp: datetime\n    value: Decimal\n    unit: str\n```\n\n### LLM Role: NONE\n\nTier 1 is pure code. LLM never touches it. Calculations like P/E, ROE, DCF use deterministic formulas in `packages/domain/fundamental/services/`.\n\n### Lead Time\n\n- Intraday: real-time (if subscribed) or 15-min delay\n- EOD: end of trading day\n- Fundamentals: filing date + ingestion lag (typically T+1 day)\n\n### Reliability\n\nVery high, BUT:\n- Source disagreements happen (Rule 4 in financial-data-protocol.md)\n- Restated financials happen (track via `restated_at` field)\n- Adjustment type confusion is biggest risk\n\n## B.2 Tier 2: Official Narrative\n\n### Sources\n\n**Required**:\n- CafeF (cafef.vn) — most popular VN financial news\n- Vietstock News (vietstock.vn) — second most popular\n- NDH (nhipcaudautu.vn) — quality analysis\n- VietnamBiz (vietnambiz.vn) — business focus\n- Đầu Tư Chứng Khoán (tinnhanhchungkhoan.vn)\n\n**Source-specific (companies/sectors)**:\n- Company official announcements via HOSE/HNX disclosure portals\n- Broker reports (SSI, HSC, VCSC, VND, FPTS) — public summaries\n\n**Government/Regulatory**:\n- SBV (State Bank of Vietnam) announcements\n- MoF (Ministry of Finance) policy documents\n- Quốc hội / Chính phủ document portals\n\n### Data Categories\n\n```python\n# Stored in BC-5 News Stream\nclass NewsArticle:\n    article_id: str\n    source: str               # \"cafef\", \"vietstock\", etc\n    url: str\n    title: str\n    body_excerpt: str         # full body in R2\n    published_at: datetime\n    ingested_at: datetime\n    \nclass ExtractedClaim:\n    claim_id: str\n    article_id: str\n    claim_text: str           # extracted by LLM\n    mentioned_tickers: list[Ticker]\n    mentioned_sectors: list[Sector]\n    sentiment: Sentiment      # categorical, not numeric (Rule 7)\n    extractor_model: str\n    extractor_version: str\n    extracted_at: datetime\n    source_text_excerpt: str\n    confidence_extracted: float\n```\n\n### LLM Role\n\n- **Extract structured claims** from unstructured news\n- **Classify sentiment** categorically (STRONGLY_BULLISH | BULLISH | NEUTRAL | BEARISH | STRONGLY_BEARISH)\n- **Identify mentioned entities** (tickers, sectors, people)\n- **Detect contradictions** between sources reporting same event\n- **Cluster related stories** (same event covered by multiple sources)\n\nLLM does NOT:\n- Generate sentiment scores numerically\n- Predict price impact (that's BC-8 Analysis with deterministic models)\n- Produce summaries that displace original (copyright + accuracy)\n\n### Lead Time\n\nReal-time. Pipeline polls every 5-15 min for new articles.\n\n### Reliability\n\nHigh but variable:\n- Major outlets (CafeF, Vietstock): high reliability for facts, watch for \"PR disguised as news\"\n- Smaller outlets: variable; calibrate per source over time\n- Broker reports: have inherent bias (broker has positions); track which broker is right for which sector\n- Government docs: high reliability for facts, low for interpretation\n\n## B.3 Tier 3: Influence Network ⭐\n\n### Sources\n\n**YouTube channels** (selected, not exhaustive — examples):\n- Mr. X30, Học Viện Đầu Tư, Take Profit, Đầu Tư Từ Đầu, Giao Dịch Cùng Tôi, etc.\n- Each channel: subscribe with yt-dlp, monitor for new uploads daily\n\n**Facebook fanpages** (public, ToS-respecting):\n- Fanpages of well-known finance KOLs\n- Read-only access, respect rate limits\n\n**Telegram public channels**:\n- Public broadcast channels (not private groups)\n- Use Telegram API with read-only bot\n\n**Podcasts**:\n- BizPodcast, Money Talk, others\n- RSS feeds + Whisper transcription\n\n**Personal blogs / Substack**:\n- Vietnamese investing bloggers with consistent output\n\n### Data Categories\n\n```python\n# Stored in BC-6 Influence Network\nclass Kol:\n    kol_id: KolId\n    name: str\n    primary_channel: ChannelId\n    secondary_channels: list[ChannelId]\n    profile_summary: str       # human-curated\n    sectors_covered: list[Sector]\n    style: KolStyle           # FUNDAMENTAL | TECHNICAL | NARRATIVE | PUMPER | MIXED\n    credibility_score: CredibilityScore  # see calibration spec\n    \nclass Channel:\n    channel_id: ChannelId\n    platform: Platform        # YOUTUBE | FACEBOOK | TELEGRAM | PODCAST | BLOG\n    url: str\n    kol_id: KolId\n    subscriber_count: int\n    last_scraped_at: datetime\n    \nclass Recommendation:\n    recommendation_id: str\n    kol_id: KolId\n    channel_id: ChannelId\n    source_url: str\n    timestamp_in_source: str   # for video: \"12:34-13:02\"\n    transcript_excerpt: str\n    ticker: Ticker\n    extracted_intent: Intent   # STRONG_BUY | BUY | WATCH | NEUTRAL | AVOID | STRONG_AVOID\n    extracted_conditions: list[str]\n    extracted_timeframe: Timeframe\n    extraction_confidence: float\n    published_at: datetime\n    extracted_at: datetime\n    \nclass CredibilityScore:\n    kol_id: KolId\n    overall_hit_rate: float\n    n_recommendations_evaluated: int\n    sector_specific_scores: dict[Sector, float]\n    timeframe_specific_scores: dict[Timeframe, float]\n    last_updated_at: datetime\n    bayesian_confidence_interval: tuple[float, float]\n```\n\n### LLM Role\n\n- **Transcribe** audio/video (Whisper, not LLM but adjacent)\n- **Extract recommendations** with timestamp, intent, conditions, timeframe\n- **Detect coordinated messaging** (same KOL mentioning many stocks in pattern)\n- **Classify KOL style** (fundamental? technical? narrative-driven? potential pumper?)\n- **Identify when KOL changes view** (was bullish, now bearish — track flip-flop frequency)\n\n### Lead Time\n\nVariable per KOL:\n- Some KOLs lead price by 1-3 days (publish recommendation, audience acts)\n- Some are coincident (publish during the move)\n- Some are contrarian indicators (pumpers; their recommendation = sell signal)\n\nThis is exactly what calibration database tracks.\n\n### Reliability\n\nVery wide range. Each KOL gets calibrated:\n- Some KOLs have 60-70% hit rate → high signal\n- Some have 30-40% → noise\n- Some have <30% → contra-indicator (consistent enough to bet against)\n\nAfter 6 months tracking, you have calibrated weights per KOL. After 12 months, statistically meaningful.\n\nSee `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` for full implementation.\n\n## B.4 Tier 4: Crowd Sentiment & Pump Detection ⭐\n\n### Sources\n\n**Forums (public)**:\n- F319 (forum cổ phiếu)\n- VFPress\n- Comments on CafeF/Vietstock articles\n\n**Facebook public groups** (public, ToS-respecting):\n- \"Đầu tư chứng khoán Việt Nam\" type groups (large public ones)\n- Read-only, respect rate limits\n- NEVER private groups\n\n**Telegram public broadcast channels**:\n- Stock-specific public channels\n- Public chat rooms only\n\n**Twitter/X**:\n- VN finance Twitter (smaller but growing)\n\n### Data Categories\n\n```python\n# Stored in BC-7 Crowd Sentiment\nclass SentimentSnapshot:\n    snapshot_id: str\n    ticker: Ticker\n    captured_at: datetime\n    window: Window               # 1H | 4H | 1D | 1W\n    mention_count: int\n    sentiment_distribution: SentimentDistribution  # categorical counts\n    posting_velocity: float       # posts/hour\n    unique_posters: int\n    coordinated_posting_score: float  # 0-1, see pump detection\n    sources: dict[str, int]       # source -> mention count\n\nclass Narrative:\n    narrative_id: NarrativeId\n    description: str               # \"đầu tư công 2026\"\n    affected_tickers: list[Ticker]\n    affected_sectors: list[Sector]\n    first_detected_at: datetime\n    current_phase: NarrativePhase  # INCUBATION | EMERGING | MAINSTREAM | SATURATION | EXHAUSTION | REVERSAL\n    velocity_trend: str            # ACCELERATING | STEADY | DECELERATING\n    counter_narratives: list[NarrativeId]\n    \nclass PumpDetection:\n    detection_id: str\n    ticker: Ticker\n    detected_at: datetime\n    phase: PumpPhase  # PRE_PUMP | PUMP | DISTRIBUTION | DUMP | UNCERTAIN\n    phase_confidence: float\n    contributing_signals: list[SignalSnapshot]  # what triggered detection\n    historical_similar_cases: list[PumpDetectionId]  # past matches\n```\n\n### LLM Role\n\n- **Classify Vietnamese sentiment** (categorical, not numeric)\n- **Detect coordinated posting** (template detection, timing clustering)\n- **Identify narrative themes** in posts\n- **Extract pump signatures** (specific language patterns)\n- **Compare to historical labeled pump cases**\n\n### Lead Time\n\nTypically LAGS price. Crowd sentiment is mostly exhaustion signal:\n- High crowd activity often = late stage\n- Sentiment euphoria often = top forming\n- Sentiment despair often = bottom forming\n\nUseful primarily as **timing signal** (when narrative-driven move is exhausting), not as **direction signal**.\n\n### Reliability\n\nMass behavior is noisy individually but structured statistically. Patterns reliable, individual posts not.\n\nSee `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` for full implementation.\n\n## B.5 Cross-Tier Synthesis\n\n### The Confluence Engine\n\nCombines tier signals into actionable assessments. Lives in BC-8 Analysis.\n\n```python\nclass ConfluenceAssessment:\n    ticker: Ticker\n    assessed_at: datetime\n    tier_signals: dict[SignalTier, TierSignal]\n    confluence_pattern: ConfluencePattern  # see patterns A.3\n    confluence_strength: float  # how many tiers agree\n    historical_pattern_matches: list[PatternMatch]\n    historical_hit_rate: float | None  # if pattern has enough samples\n    recommended_action: RecommendedAction  # WATCH | INVESTIGATE | THESIS_CANDIDATE | PUMP_AVOID\n    reasoning: str  # LLM-generated, grounded in tier signals\n```\n\n### Decision Rules\n\nPhase 1: Simple rules\n- All 4 tiers agree positive → WATCH (potential thesis)\n- T1+T2+T3 agree, T4 silent → INVESTIGATE (early opportunity)\n- T4 hot, T1 weak → AVOID (potential top)\n- T3 hot from low-credibility KOLs only, T1 weak → PUMP_AVOID\n\nPhase 4: Karpathy-optimized weights\n- Outer loop tunes signal weights based on historical hit rate\n- Same patterns evaluated, but weights evolve\n\n## B.6 Storage Strategy\n\n### Tier 1 (high volume, time-series)\n- TimescaleDB hypertables (price/volume)\n- Postgres regular tables (fundamentals)\n- Compression: TimescaleDB native after 7 days\n- Retention: full history for prices, 10 years for fundamentals\n\n### Tier 2 (medium volume, append-mostly)\n- Postgres for metadata + extracted claims\n- R2 for full article text (cheap storage)\n- pgvector for semantic search across articles\n- Retention: full history (cheap)\n\n### Tier 3 (medium volume, mixed)\n- Postgres for KOL profiles + recommendations + calibration\n- R2 for video/podcast transcripts\n- Retention: full history of recommendations + calibrated outcomes\n\n### Tier 4 (high volume, low individual value)\n- TimescaleDB for sentiment snapshots\n- Postgres for narratives + pump detections\n- R2 for raw posts (sample only, not all)\n- Retention: aggregated retained forever, individual posts 90 days unless flagged\n\n## B.7 Update Cadences\n\n```yaml\n# Defined in apps/data-pipeline/schedules.py\n\ntier_1_hard_data:\n  intraday: every 15 min during market hours (9:00-15:00 VN time)\n  eod: 16:00 daily\n  fundamentals: nightly poll for new filings\n  macro: daily at 8:00\n\ntier_2_official_narrative:\n  news_polling: every 10 min\n  broker_reports: daily at 18:00\n  regulatory: every 4 hours\n\ntier_3_influence_network:\n  youtube_channels: daily at 20:00 (after most channels publish)\n  facebook_fanpages: every 4 hours during active hours\n  podcasts: hourly RSS check\n  blogs: every 2 hours\n\ntier_4_crowd_sentiment:\n  forums: every 30 min\n  facebook_groups: every 2 hours\n  telegram: every 1 hour\n  comments: with parent article fetch\n\ncross_tier:\n  confluence_engine: every 1 hour during market days\n  pattern_matching: nightly at 22:00\n  narrative_lifecycle_update: every 4 hours\n```\n\n## B.8 Cost Profile\n\nEstimated costs for Phase 1 (Tier 1+2 only) for personal use:\n\n```\nData sources: 0-2M VND/month (mostly free + light scraping)\nLLM (Tier 2 extraction): ~5-10M VND/month\nCompute (single VPS): 1-2M VND/month\nStorage (R2): <0.5M VND/month\nTotal: ~10-15M VND/month\n```\n\nPhase 2 adds Tier 3+4:\n```\n+Whisper transcription: 3-5M VND/month\n+LLM (Tier 3+4 extraction): 10-15M VND/month\n+Storage growth: 1-2M VND/month\nTotal: ~25-35M VND/month\n```\n\n## B.9 Privacy & Legal\n\n- All scraping respects robots.txt and reasonable rate limits\n- User agent identifies as research tool with contact email\n- Public sources only — never private groups, never paid leaks\n- Personal data not retained beyond what's needed for analysis\n- GDPR-style: if KOL requests their data removed, comply\n- Output explicitly framed as personal research, never licensed advice\n\n---\n\n# PART C — PROVENANCE & REVIEW\n\n## C.1 Why This Architecture (Not Alternative)\n\n**Considered alternative**: Single-pipeline LLM approach (feed everything to LLM, get verdict)\n- **Rejected because**: black box, no calibration possible, no compounding moat, hallucination risk extreme\n\n**Considered alternative**: Pure quant (no LLM, no sentiment)\n- **Rejected because**: misses VN-specific edge, gets value-trapped, can't detect pumps\n\n**Considered alternative**: Three-tier (combine T3+T4)\n- **Rejected because**: Influence Network and Crowd Sentiment have very different ingestion patterns, calibration logic, and meaning. Combining creates god-package. Separation enables independent evolution.\n\n**Considered alternative**: Five-tier (split T1 into market data + fundamentals)\n- **Rejected because**: They're both deterministic with same LLM role (none); the natural split is BC-1 vs BC-2 in architecture, not two separate signal tiers.\n\n## C.2 Open Questions for Future Revision\n\n- Should there be a Tier 5 for \"insider\" signals (block trades, unusual options activity)? Currently in BC-1 but possibly distinct.\n- Should foreign flow get its own tier? Currently in BC-1 Market Data.\n- How do we handle paid data sources cleanly when they enter (FiinPro)?\n- When does Karpathy outer loop become reliable enough to trust weight updates?\n\n## C.3 Reviews\n\n| Date | Reviewer | Status | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 2026-04-23 | project-owner | Approved | Initial spec |\n\n---\n\n## Related Specs\n\n- **Implementation specs (Tier 2)**:\n  - `specs/tier2-feature/001-validate-investment-thesis.md` — uses confluence engine\n  - `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` — implements Tier 3\n  - `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` — implements Tier 4\n  - `specs/tier2-feature/004-multi-perspective-adversarial-agents.md` — synthesizes tiers\n  - `specs/tier2-feature/005-karpathy-outer-loop.md` — optimizes tier weights\n\n- **Constitution**:\n  - `agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md` — 9 BC structure\n  - `agent-workspace/constitution/financial-data-protocol.md` — data integrity rules\n  - `agent-workspace/constitution/invariants.md` — I-S* stock-specific invariants\n","numLines":611,"startLine":1,"totalLines":611}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f3924ec7-f585-4e42-93a8-cf03010b3074","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"d97632a7-61c5-43c2-9039-fd6ba70fbfee","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"c8e227df-a41f-4018-a3a8-74fa5fc2a9e5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"0e831e8a-4c8d-4f72-819f-519f0d2f6fbe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FXoKLxT2KMV4aDdm7AFQjD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tspec_id: SPEC-2026-04-23-001\n3\ttier: 2\n4\tstatus: approved\n5\tversion: 1.0\n6\tcreated: 2026-04-23\n7\tauthors: [project-owner]\n8\tbounded_contexts: [Analysis, Market Data, Fundamental, News Stream]\n9\trelated_specs: [SPEC-2026-04-23-T1-001, SPEC-2026-04-23-004]\n10\tubiquitous_language_terms: [Thesis, Ticker, Thesis Card, Recommendation, Point-in-Time, Fair Value Range, Trade-Off Matrix]\n11\t---\n12\t\n13\t# SPEC: Validate Investment Thesis (First End-to-End Feature)\n14\t\n15\t> **This is the first feature you build**. Thin slice spanning BC-1, BC-2, BC-5, and BC-8.\n16\t> Gives end-to-end workflow from ticker → thesis card. Other features enhance this core.\n17\t\n18\t---\n19\t\n20\t# PART A — BUSINESS SPECIFICATION\n21\t\n22\t## A.1 Why This Feature First\n23\t\n24\tGoal of Phase 1: a thin slice that touches every layer of the system, delivers dogfoodable value, and exposes integration issues early. Thesis validation is that slice because:\n25\t\n26\t1. **It's the primary use case** — answers \"should I look deeper at this stock?\"\n27\t2. **It spans 4 BCs** — forces architecture decisions early\n28\t3. **It's personally valuable Day 1** — even without KOL tracking or pump detection\n29\t4. **It gives eval data** — every thesis becomes evaluation for later outer loop\n30\t\n31\t**Phase 1 version is intentionally minimal**:\n32\t- Tier 1 + Tier 2 data only (no KOL, no crowd sentiment yet)\n33\t- 3 perspectives only: Bear, Bull, Quant (add Macro, Behavior, Manager in Phase 3)\n34\t- Synthesizer with simple trade-off matrix\n35\t- Streamlit dashboard interface\n36\t\n37\t**Phase 3 expansion** (after 004 fully built):\n38\t- 6 perspectives\n39\t- Counter-narrative integration\n40\t- Calibration-based confidence\n41\t\n42\t## A.2 User Story\n43\t\n44\t```\n45\tAs the project owner, when I want to evaluate whether to investigate HPG further,\n46\tI want to type \"HPG\" into StockForge and get a structured thesis card within 5\n47\tminutes that surfaces bull case, bear case, valuation, and what I should\n48\tinvestigate further — not a buy/sell signal.\n49\t```\n50\t\n51\t## A.3 Acceptance Criteria\n52\t\n53\t**AC-1: End-to-End Workflow**\n54\t- Input: ticker symbol (e.g., \"HPG\")\n55\t- Output: thesis card with sections defined in Section A.4\n56\t- Duration: ≤5 minutes from input to rendered card\n57\t- Cost: ≤$3 per validation (tracking enforced)\n58\t\n59\t**AC-2: All Output Grounded**\n60\t- Every claim has source URL\n61\t- Every number traces to tool call (no LLM-generated numbers)\n62\t- As-of dates visible on all data\n63\t\n64\t**AC-3: Bear Case Substantive**\n65\t- ≥3 distinct bear points\n66\t- Each with specific evidence (not boilerplate)\n67\t- System refuses to render thesis without substantive bear case\n68\t\n69\t**AC-4: Multi-Source Disagreement Surfaced**\n70\t- If Bear and Bull perspectives reach opposite conclusions with equal conviction, thesis shows DISAGREEMENT explicitly\n71\t- Final recommendation is INVESTIGATE (not forced consensus)\n72\t\n73\t**AC-5: Reproducibility**\n74\t- Same ticker + as-of date = same thesis (with same model version + prompt hash)\n75\t- Thesis ID + metadata stored for audit\n76\t\n77\t**AC-6: Disclaimer Present**\n78\t- Output includes research-aid disclaimer (I-S35)\n79\t\n80\t## A.4 Thesis Card Sections (Phase 1 minimal)\n81\t\n82\tOutput format (markdown), rendered in Streamlit:\n83\t\n84\t```markdown\n85\t# Thesis Card: {TICKER} (as of {AS_OF_DATE})\n86\t\n87\t## Summary\n88\t**Recommendation**: {THESIS_CANDIDATE | INVESTIGATE | WATCH | PASS}\n89\t**Confidence**: {HIGH | MEDIUM | LOW}\n90\t**Top Question to Resolve**: {one-sentence key uncertainty}\n91\t\n92\t## Trade-Off Matrix\n93\t| Dimension | Verdict | Key Evidence |\n94\t|---|---|---|\n95\t| Value | {STRONG/NEUTRAL/WEAK} | {grounded evidence} |\n96\t| Quality | ... | ... |\n97\t| Growth | ... | ... |\n98\t| Risk | ... | ... |\n99\t\n100\t## Quant Summary (numbers from code, not LLM)\n101\t- **Current Price**: {price} as of {timestamp}\n102\t- **Fair Value Range**: {low} — {mid} — {high}\n103\t  - DCF (WACC {x}%, g {y}%): {value}\n104\t  - Graham Number: {value}\n105\t  - Peer multiples (P/E, P/B avg): implied {value}\n106\t- **Key Ratios (TTM)**:\n107\t  - P/E: {x} (sector avg {y}, 5-yr own avg {z})\n108\t  - P/B: {x}\n109\t  - ROE: {x}% (sector avg {y}%)\n110\t  - Debt/Equity: {x}\n111\t- **Margin of Safety** (vs mid fair value): {x}%\n112\t\n113\t## Bull Case ({N} points)\n114\t1. **{Point}** — {grounded evidence + source URL}\n115\t2. ...\n116\t\n117\t## Bear Case ({N} points, minimum 3)\n118\t1. **{Point}** — {grounded evidence + source URL}\n119\t2. ...\n120\t3. ...\n121\t\n122\t## Explicit Disagreements\n123\t{List of topics where Bear and Bull disagree substantively, with each position summarized}\n124\tOR\n125\t{\"No substantive disagreements — perspectives align\"}\n126\t\n127\t## Catalysts (what could trigger re-rating)\n128\t- {catalyst} ({timeframe}, {likelihood})\n129\t\n130\t## Risks (what could invalidate thesis)\n131\t- {risk} ({impact level})\n132\t\n133\t## Data Freshness\n134\t- Price data: {staleness indicator}\n135\t- Fundamentals: {as of {period_end}, filed {filing_date}}\n136\t- News: {most recent article date}\n137\t\n138\t## Reasoning Trace\n139\t{How synthesizer arrived at recommendation, citing which perspectives support what}\n140\t\n141\t---\n142\t*This is a research aid, not financial advice. Decisions and responsibility are yours.*\n143\t\n144\t[Archive Thesis ID: {thesis_id}]\n145\t```\n146\t\n147\t## A.5 Out of Scope (Phase 1)\n148\t\n149\t- KOL recommendation integration (Phase 2, spec 002)\n150\t- Crowd sentiment integration (Phase 2, spec 003)\n151\t- Counter-narrative generation (Phase 3)\n152\t- Macro / Manager / Behavior perspectives (Phase 3, spec 004 expansion)\n153\t- Outcome tracking (Phase 2)\n154\t- Portfolio/position integration (Phase 4)\n155\t- Multi-ticker comparison (later)\n156\t\n157\t---\n158\t\n159\t# PART B — AGENT CONTRACT\n160\t\n161\t## B.1 Bounded Context Involvement\n162\t\n163\t| BC | Role | What It Provides |\n164\t|---|---|---|\n165\t| BC-1 Market Data | Input | Current price, volume, foreign flow |\n166\t| BC-2 Fundamental | Input | Financial statements, ratios, peer data |\n167\t| BC-5 News Stream | Input | Recent news, sentiment (Phase 1 simple) |\n168\t| BC-8 Analysis | Orchestrator | Thesis aggregate, perspective coordination, synthesis |\n169\t\n170\t## B.2 Phase 1 Simplifications\n171\t\n172\t**Perspectives Phase 1**: only 3 agents (Bear, Bull, Quant). Macro/Behavior/Manager stubbed out (return empty or marked \"Phase 3\").\n173\t\n174\t**Calibration Phase 1**: no historical hit rate yet. Confidence set heuristically:\n175\t- HIGH: all 3 perspectives strong conviction AND agree\n176\t- MEDIUM: perspectives mixed but evidence grounded\n177\t- LOW: perspectives disagree OR evidence limited\n178\t\n179\t**Data gathering Phase 1**: automated via BC-1, BC-2, BC-5 adapters. No LLM-driven search yet.\n180\t\n181\t## B.3 Key Use Case Implementation\n182\t\n183\t```python\n184\t# packages/application/analysis/use_cases/validate_thesis_phase1.py\n185\t\n186\tclass ValidateThesisPhase1UseCase:\n187\t    \"\"\"Phase 1 version of thesis validation.\n188\t    \n189\t    Simplifications from full spec (004):\n190\t    - 3 perspectives only (Bear/Bull/Quant)\n191\t    - No counter-narrative integration\n192\t    - No calibration-based confidence\n193\t    - Bias log stubbed\n194\t    \n195\t    Future: upgrade to full 004 version once all BCs exist.\n196\t    \"\"\"\n197\t    \n198\t    def __init__(\n199\t        self,\n200\t        data_gatherer: Phase1DataGatherer,\n201\t        bear_agent: BearPerspectiveAgent,\n202\t        bull_agent: BullPerspectiveAgent,\n203\t        quant_agent: QuantPerspectiveAgent,\n204\t        synthesizer: Phase1Synthesizer,\n205\t        thesis_repo: ThesisRepository,\n206\t        event_bus: EventBus,\n207\t        cost_tracker: CostTrackerPort,\n208\t    ):\n209\t        ...\n210\t    \n211\t    async def execute(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of: date | None = None) -> Thesis:\n212\t        as_of = as_of or date.today()\n213\t        thesis_id = new_id()\n214\t        \n215\t        # Cost budget enforcement\n216\t        with self.cost_tracker.scoped_budget(limit_usd=3.0) as budget:\n217\t            # Step 1: Gather Tier 1+2 data (deterministic, no LLM)\n218\t            context = await self.data_gatherer.gather(ticker, as_of)\n219\t            if context.has_critical_gaps():\n220\t                return Thesis.incomplete(\n221\t                    thesis_id=thesis_id, ticker=ticker,\n222\t                    reason=f\"missing critical data: {context.gaps}\",\n223\t                )\n224\t            \n225\t            # Step 2: Run 3 perspectives in parallel\n226\t            bear_task = self.bear_agent.analyze(ticker, context)\n227\t            bull_task = self.bull_agent.analyze(ticker, context)\n228\t            quant_task = self.quant_agent.analyze(ticker, context)\n229\t            \n230\t            perspectives = await asyncio.gather(bear_task, bull_task, quant_task)\n231\t            \n232\t            # Step 3: Synthesize (preserve disagreements)\n233\t            synthesis = await self.synthesizer.synthesize(\n234\t                ticker=ticker, perspectives=perspectives, context=context,\n235\t            )\n236\t            \n237\t            # Step 4: Determine recommendation (heuristic Phase 1)\n238\t            recommendation = self._recommendation_from_synthesis(synthesis)\n239\t            confidence = self._confidence_from_synthesis(synthesis)\n240\t            \n241\t            # Step 5: Build thesis (aggregate invariants validate)\n242\t            thesis = Thesis(\n243\t                thesis_id=thesis_id,\n244\t                ticker=ticker,\n245\t                created_at=datetime.utcnow(),\n246\t                as_of=as_of,\n247\t                user_intent=None,\n248\t                perspectives=perspectives,\n249\t                synthesis=synthesis,\n250\t                final_recommendation=recommendation,\n251\t                confidence_level=confidence,\n252\t                status=ThesisStatus.DRAFT,\n253\t            )\n254\t            await self.thesis_repo.save(thesis)\n255\t            await self.event_bus.publish(ThesisRecorded(\n256\t                thesis_id=thesis_id, ticker=ticker, recommendation=recommendation,\n257\t                recorded_at=datetime.utcnow(),\n258\t            ))\n259\t            \n260\t            return thesis\n261\t    \n262\t    def _recommendation_from_synthesis(self, synth: Synthesis) -> Recommendation:\n263\t        # Heuristic Phase 1 rules:\n264\t        # THESIS_CANDIDATE: all dimensions positive, no substantive disagreement\n265\t        # PASS: all dimensions negative OR governance red flag\n266\t        # WATCH: mostly neutral or mixed-weak\n267\t        # INVESTIGATE (default): substantive signals present but disagreement or key question unresolved\n268\t        ...\n269\t    \n270\t    def _confidence_from_synthesis(self, synth: Synthesis) -> ConfidenceLevel:\n271\t        # Phase 1 heuristic; Phase 3 replaces with calibration-based\n272\t        ...\n273\t```\n274\t\n275\t## B.4 Phase 1 Data Gatherer\n276\t\n277\t```python\n278\t# packages/infrastructure/analysis/phase1_data_gatherer.py\n279\t\n280\tclass Phase1DataGatherer:\n281\t    \"\"\"Assembles context for Phase 1 thesis validation.\n282\t    \n283\t    Pulls from BC-1 (prices), BC-2 (fundamentals), BC-5 (news).\n284\t    Returns SharedContext with all data + staleness indicators.\n285\t    \"\"\"\n286\t    \n287\t    async def gather(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of: date) -> SharedContext:\n288\t        # All queries use point-in-time (I-S2)\n289\t        quotes = await self.quote_repo.get_range_as_of(\n290\t            ticker=ticker, from_date=as_of - timedelta(days=365), to_date=as_of,\n291\t        )\n292\t        financials = await self.fundamental_repo.get_as_of(ticker, as_of)\n293\t        ratios_ttm = self.ratio_service.compute_ttm_ratios(financials, quotes)\n294\t        peer_comparables = await self.peer_service.get_comparables(ticker, sector_as_of=as_of)\n295\t        historical_ratios = self.ratio_service.compute_historical_percentiles(\n296\t            ticker, quotes, financials, years=5,\n297\t        )\n298\t        recent_news = await self.news_repo.get_for_ticker(\n299\t            ticker, from_date=as_of - timedelta(days=90), to_date=as_of,\n300\t        )\n301\t        sector_news = await self.news_repo.get_for_sector(\n302\t            sector=financials.sector, from_date=as_of - timedelta(days=90),\n303\t        )\n304\t        \n305\t        # Staleness check\n306\t        gaps = []\n307\t        if not quotes or quotes[-1].date < as_of - timedelta(days=3):\n308\t            gaps.append(\"price_data_stale\")\n309\t        if not financials or financials.filing_date < as_of - timedelta(days=180):\n310\t            gaps.append(\"fundamentals_stale\")\n311\t        if len(recent_news) == 0:\n312\t            gaps.append(\"no_news_last_90d\")\n313\t        \n314\t        return SharedContext(\n315\t            ticker=ticker, as_of=as_of,\n316\t            quotes=quotes, financials=financials, ratios_ttm=ratios_ttm,\n317\t            peer_comparables=peer_comparables, historical_ratios=historical_ratios,\n318\t            recent_news=recent_news, sector_news=sector_news,\n319\t            gaps=gaps,\n320\t        )\n321\t```\n322\t\n323\t## B.5 Phase 1 Bear/Bull Agents (abbreviated)\n324\t\n325\t```python\n326\t# packages/infrastructure/analysis/perspectives/bear_agent.py\n327\t\n328\tclass BearPerspectiveAgent:\n329\t    \"\"\"Phase 1 Bear agent — operates on SharedContext only, no deep search.\"\"\"\n330\t\n331\t    async def analyze(self, ticker: Ticker, context: SharedContext) -> PerspectiveAnalysis:\n332\t        tool_set = BearToolSet(context)\n333\t        \n334\t        system_prompt = self._build_system_prompt(ticker)\n335\t        response = await self.llm.complete(\n336\t            system_prompt=system_prompt,\n337\t            messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": f\"Analyze {ticker}\"}],\n338\t            tools=tool_set.tools,\n339\t            model=\"claude-sonnet-4-6\",\n340\t        )\n341\t        \n342\t        # Parse structured output — must include key_points with source_urls\n343\t        analysis = self._parse_response(response, role=PerspectiveRole.BEAR)\n344\t        \n345\t        # Enforce BR-3 (substantive bear case)\n346\t        if len(analysis.key_points) < 3:\n347\t            # Retry with more explicit instruction\n348\t            analysis = await self._retry_with_bear_minimum(ticker, context, analysis)\n349\t        \n350\t        return analysis\n351\t    \n352\t    def _build_system_prompt(self, ticker: Ticker) -> str:\n353\t        return f\"\"\"You are a BEAR analyst for {ticker}. Your job is to find every\n354\tcredible reason NOT to buy this stock. You are NOT balanced. Adversarial role.\n355\t\n356\tRules:\n357\t- Every claim cites source (URL + excerpt from context data)\n358\t- Focus on specific, material risks — not boilerplate\n359\t- Required categories: fundamental concerns, structural risks, valuation concerns,\n360\t  competitive threats\n361\t- Minimum 3 substantive points, each grounded\n362\t- Rate your conviction (STRONG/MODERATE/WEAK)\n363\t- Do NOT generate numbers in prose — use tool calls; state computed results only\n364\t- If unable to find substantive bear case after examining context, state so\n365\t\n366\tOutput as JSON matching PerspectiveAnalysis schema.\n367\t\"\"\"\n368\t```\n369\t\n370\tSame pattern for BullPerspectiveAgent and QuantPerspectiveAgent, with role-appropriate system prompts.\n371\t\n372\t## B.6 Phase 1 Synthesizer\n373\t\n374\t```python\n375\t# packages/infrastructure/analysis/phase1_synthesizer.py\n376\t\n377\tclass Phase1Synthesizer:\n378\t    \"\"\"Synthesizes 3 perspective analyses into trade-off matrix.\n379\t    \n380\t    KEY: does NOT collapse disagreement. Preserves it.\n381\t    \"\"\"\n382\t\n383\t    async def synthesize(\n384\t        self, ticker: Ticker, perspectives: list[PerspectiveAnalysis], context: SharedContext,\n385\t    ) -> Synthesis:\n386\t        # Build trade-off matrix across standard dimensions\n387\t        trade_off = TradeOffMatrix(dimensions=[VALUE, QUALITY, GROWTH, RISK])\n388\t        for dim in trade_off.dimensions:\n389\t            trade_off.scores[dim] = self._assess_dimension(dim, perspectives)\n390\t        \n391\t        # Explicit disagreement detection\n392\t        disagreements = self._detect_disagreements(perspectives)\n393\t        \n394\t        # Catalysts from bull + risks from bear (both grounded)\n395\t        catalysts = [c for p in perspectives if p.role == PerspectiveRole.BULL \n396\t                     for c in self._extract_catalysts(p)]\n397\t        risks = [r for p in perspectives if p.role == PerspectiveRole.BEAR\n398\t                 for r in self._extract_risks(p)]\n399\t        \n400\t        # Reasoning trace via LLM (grounded)\n401\t        reasoning = await self._generate_reasoning_trace(\n402\t            ticker, perspectives, trade_off, disagreements,\n403\t        )\n404\t        \n405\t        return Synthesis(\n406\t            trade_off_matrix=trade_off,\n407\t            confluence_assessment=self._confluence(perspectives),\n408\t            explicit_disagreements=disagreements,\n409\t            catalysts=catalysts,\n410\t            risks=risks,\n411\t            personal_bias_flags=[],  # Phase 1 stub\n412\t            counter_narrative_summary=None,  # Phase 1 stub\n413\t            reasoning_trace=reasoning,\n414\t        )\n415\t```\n416\t\n417\t## B.7 Streamlit UI\n418\t\n419\tSingle page for Phase 1:\n420\t\n421\t```python\n422\t# apps/dashboard/pages/validate_thesis.py\n423\t\n424\timport streamlit as st\n425\t\n426\tst.title(\"Validate st.button(\"Validate\"):\n432\t    with st.spinner(f\"Running thesis validation on {ticker}...\"):\n433\t        thesis = await validate_thesis_usecase.execute(ticker, as_of)\n434\t    \n435\t    # Render thesis card\n436\t    render_thesis_card(thesis)\n437\t    st.caption(f\"Thesis ID: {thesis.thesis_id} • Cost: ${thesis.cost_usd:.3f}\")\n438\t```\n439\t\n440\t## B.8 Tasks (concrete work items)\n441\t\n442\t**Week 1 — Domain Foundation**\n443\t- T-001: Implement BC-8 domain models: `Thesis`, `PerspectiveAnalysis`, `Synthesis`, `TradeOffMatrix`, `GroundedPoint`\n444\t- T-002: Implement T-004: Postgres schema for `theses` + `perspective_analyses`\n449\t- T-005: `QuoteRepository`, `FundamentalRepository` with point-in-time methods\n450\t- T-006: vnstock adapter for Quote ingestion (Tier 1)\n451\t- T-007: CafeF adapter for news ingestion (Tier 2)\n452\t- T-008: Phase1DataGatherer implementation\n453\t\n454\t**Week 3 — Perspective Agents**\n455\t- T-009: `BearPerspectiveAgent` Enforced\n474\t\n475\t- **I-1** every claim has source_url (perspective key_points validated)\n476\t- **I-S1** no LLM math (Quant agent only uses tool calls for numbers)\n477\t- **I-S2** point-in-time queries (repositories have no `get_latest` in this path)\n478\t- **I-S10** bear case substantive ≥3 points (aggregate validates)\n479\t- **I-S11** ≥4 perspectives (Phase 1 exception: 3 + 3 stubs = reliability) = $0.70\n492\t- Synthesizer: ~15K tokens (Opus) = $0.40\n493\t- Data gather: ~$0 (deterministic)\n494\t- Target: ~$1.30 average, <$3 ceiling\n495\t\n496\tMonthly at 20 validations: Within personal budget.\n497\t\n498\t## B.11 Success Measures (Phase 1 feature-level)\n499\t\n500\t- [ ] End-to-end validation runs <5 min on 90% of tests\n501\t- [ ] All invariants enforced (no violations in test set)\n502\t- [ ] First 10 thesis produce genuinely useful output (user opinion)\n503\t- [ ] Cost stays within budget ($3 ceiling)\n504\t- [ ] User runs validation ≥2x/week for real decisions\n505\t\n506\t---\n507\t\n508\t# PART C — PROVENANCE & REVIEW\n509\t\n510\t## C.1 Why Phase 1 Simplifications\n511\t\n512\t**Only 3 perspectives**: Macro/Behavior/Manager need BC-4/BC-7/BC-3 maturity first.
